Model before rewriting:

find x: int(5..7)
find y: int(0..3)
find z: int(0..4)

such that

x % y % z != 3

--

x
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
x
new variables:
  find x#sign_magnitude_1: bool
  find x#sign_magnitude_2: bool
  find x#sign_magnitude_3: bool
  find x#sign_magnitude_4: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(x#sign_magnitude_3),or([!(x#sign_magnitude_2),or([!(x#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])
  !(x#sign_magnitude_4)
  (!(x#sign_magnitude_4)) -> (or([!(x#sign_magnitude_3),or([!(x#sign_magnitude_2),or([!(x#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]))
  (x#sign_magnitude_4) -> (or([x#sign_magnitude_1,x#sign_magnitude_2,x#sign_magnitude_3;int(1..)]))
  or([and([SATInt(SignMagnitude, [x#sign_magnitude_1,x#sign_magnitude_2,x#sign_magnitude_3,x#sign_magnitude_4;int(1..)] [5, 7]) >= 5,SATInt(SignMagnitude, [x#sign_magnitude_1,x#sign_magnitude_2,x#sign_magnitude_3,x#sign_magnitude_4;int(1..)] [5, 7]) <= 7;int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

SATInt(SignMagnitude, [x#sign_magnitude_1,x#sign_magnitude_2,x#sign_magnitude_3,x#sign_magnitude_4;int(1..)] [5, 7]) >= 5
   ~~> select_integer_relation ([("SAT", 18500)])
__0
new variables:
  findAux __0: bool
new SAT encoding decisions:
  define(__0 <-> integer-weighted(1*x#sign_magnitude_1, -2*and([x#sign_magnitude_4,x#sign_magnitude_1;int(1..)]), 2*x#sign_magnitude_2, -4*and([x#sign_magnitude_4,x#sign_magnitude_2;int(1..)]), 4*x#sign_magnitude_3, -8*and([x#sign_magnitude_4,x#sign_magnitude_3;int(1..)])) GreaterEqual 5) using None with []

--

SATInt(SignMagnitude, [x#sign_magnitude_1,x#sign_magnitude_2,x#sign_magnitude_3,x#sign_magnitude_4;int(1..)] [5, 7]) <= 7
   ~~> select_integer_relation ([("SAT", 18500)])
__1
new variables:
  findAux __1: bool
new SAT encoding decisions:
  define(__1 <-> integer-weighted(1*x#sign_magnitude_1, -2*and([x#sign_magnitude_4,x#sign_magnitude_1;int(1..)]), 2*x#sign_magnitude_2, -4*and([x#sign_magnitude_4,x#sign_magnitude_2;int(1..)]), 4*x#sign_magnitude_3, -8*and([x#sign_magnitude_4,x#sign_magnitude_3;int(1..)])) LessEqual 7) using None with []

--

x % y % z != 3,
true,
!(x#sign_magnitude_4),
true,
(x#sign_magnitude_4) -> (or([x#sign_magnitude_1,x#sign_magnitude_2,x#sign_magnitude_3;int(1..)])),
or([and([__0,__1;int(1..)]);int(1..)])
   ~~> select_asserted_boolean ([("SAT", 18000)])
x % y % z != 3,
true,
true,
(x#sign_magnitude_4) -> (or([x#sign_magnitude_1,x#sign_magnitude_2,x#sign_magnitude_3;int(1..)])),
or([and([__0,__1;int(1..)]);int(1..)])
new SAT encoding decisions:
  assert(!(x#sign_magnitude_4)) using tseitin

--

x % y % z != 3,
true,
true,
(x#sign_magnitude_4) -> (or([x#sign_magnitude_1,x#sign_magnitude_2,x#sign_magnitude_3;int(1..)])),
or([and([__0,__1;int(1..)]);int(1..)])
   ~~> select_asserted_boolean ([("SAT", 18000)])
x % y % z != 3,
true,
true,
or([and([__0,__1;int(1..)]);int(1..)])
new SAT encoding decisions:
  assert((x#sign_magnitude_4) -> (or([x#sign_magnitude_1,x#sign_magnitude_2,x#sign_magnitude_3;int(1..)]))) using tseitin

--

x % y % z != 3,
true,
true,
or([and([__0,__1;int(1..)]);int(1..)])
   ~~> select_asserted_boolean ([("SAT", 18000)])
x % y % z != 3,
true,
true
